<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-tertiary-container/90 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
            ویژه مقاصد آنتالیا، استانبول، دبی و گرجستان با پروازهای چارتر اختصاصی کارن تراول.
          </p>

            کپی کد

<script>
  // Countdown Timer Micro-interaction (Persian Digits)
  (function initTimer() {
    let duration = 8 * 60 + 22; // 08:22 in seconds
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('checkoutTimer');
    if (!timerEl) return;

    function toPersianDigits(num) {
      const pDigits = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];
      return num.toString().replace(/[0-9]/g, (w) => pDigits[+w]);
    }

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      if (duration <= 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        timerEl.innerText = '۰۰:۰۰';
        return;
      }
      duration--;
      const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
      const seconds = duration % 60;
      const formatted = 
        String(minutes).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + 
        String(seconds).padStart(2, '0');
      timerEl.innerText = toPersianDigits(formatted);
    }, 1000);
  })();

  // Dynamic Radio Style Switcher
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="payment_method"]').forEach((radio) => {
    radio.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.payment-method-card').forEach((card) => {
        card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low/70', 'ring-2', 'ring-primary');
        card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest');
      });
      const currentCard = e.target.closest('.payment-method-card');
      if (currentCard) {
        currentCard.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest');
        currentCard.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low/70');
      }
    });
  });
</script></main><footer class="w-full bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface mt-space-xl pt-space-xl pb-space-lg rounded-t-[36px]"><div class="max-w-[1320px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop"><div <script>
  // Countdown Timer Micro-interaction (Persian Digits)
  (function initTimer() {
    let duration = 8 * 60 + 22; // 08:22 in seconds
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('checkoutTimer');
    if (!timerEl) return;

    function toPersianDigits(num) {
      const pDigits = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];
      return num.toString().replace(/[0-9]/g, (w) => pDigits[+w]);
    }

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      if (duration <= 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        timerEl.innerText = '۰۰:۰۰';
        return;
      }
      duration--;
      const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
      const seconds = duration % 60;
      const formatted = 
        String(minutes).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + 
        String(seconds).padStart(2, '0');
      timerEl.innerText = toPersianDigits(formatted);
    }, 1000);
  })();

  // Dynamic Radio Style Switcher
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="payment_method"]').forEach((radio) => {
    radio.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.payment-method-card').forEach((card) => {
        card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low/70', 'ring-2', 'ring-primary');
        card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest');
      });
      const currentCard = e.target.closest('.payment-method-card');
      if (currentCard) {
        currentCard.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest');
        currentCard.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low/70');
      }
    });
  });
</script></main><footer class="w-full bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface mt-space-xl pt-space-xl pb-space-lg rounded-t-[36px]"><div class="max-w-[1320px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop"><div <script>
  // Countdown Timer Micro-interaction (Persian Digits)
  (function initTimer() {
    let duration = 8 * 60 + 22; // 08:22 in seconds
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('checkoutTimer');
    if (!timerEl) return;

    function toPersianDigits(num) {
      const pDigits = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];
      return num.toString().replace(/[0-9]/g, (w) => pDigits[+w]);
    }

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      if (duration <= 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        timerEl.innerText = '۰۰:۰۰';
        return;
      }
      duration--;
      const minutes = Math.floor(duration / 60);
      const seconds = duration % 60;
      const formatted = 
        String(minutes).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + 
        String(seconds).padStart(2, '0');
      timerEl.innerText = toPersianDigits(formatted);
    }, 1000);
  })();

  // Dynamic Radio Style Switcher
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="payment_method"]').forEach((radio) => {
    radio.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.payment-method-card').forEach((card) => {
        card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low/70', 'ring-2', 'ring-primary');
        card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest');
      });
      const currentCard = e.target.closest('.payment-method-card');
      if (currentCard) {
        currentCard.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest');
        currentCard.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low/70');
      }
    });
  });
</script></main><footer class="w-full bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface mt-space-xl pt-space-xl pb-space-lg rounded-t-[36px]"><div class="max-w-[1320px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop"><div <script>
  (function() {
    const chatForm = document.getElementById('chatForm');
    const chatInput = document.getElementById('chatInput');
    const chatScrollArea = document.getElementById('chatScrollArea');
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearChatBtn');
    const chipPrompts = document.querySelectorAll('.chip-prompt');

    // Fill textarea with clicked prompt chip
    chipPrompts.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        chatInput.value = chip.textContent.trim();
        chatInput.focus();
      });
    });

    // Handle new message submit
    if (chatForm && chatInput && chatScrollArea) {
      chatForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const text = chatInput.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        // Append user bubble
        const now = new Date();
        const timeStr = now.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0') + ':' + now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0');
        
        const userBubble = document.createElement('div');
        userBubble.className = 'flex items-start justify-end gap-3';
        userBubble.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5 max-w-[85%]">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">${timeStr}</span>
              <span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-semibold">کیان احمدی</span>
            </div>
            <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none bg-primary text-on-primary font-body-md text-body-md leading-relaxed shadow-md">
              ${escapeHtml(text)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-label-lg font-bold">
            ک‌ا
          </div>
        `;
        chatScrollArea.appendChild(userBubble);
        chatInput.value = '';

        // Simulate AI thinking & reply
        setTimeout(() => {
          const aiBubble = document.createElement('div');
          aiBubble.className = 'flex items-start gap-3';
          aiBubble.innerHTML = `
            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-secondary text-on-secondary flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">psychology</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 max-w-[85%]">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-semibold">دستیار کارن AI</span>
                <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">${timeStr}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md leading-relaxed shadow-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-[18px] animate-spin">sync</span>
                <span>در حال تحلیل گزینه‌ها و استعلام ظرفیت پروازها بر اساس درخواست شما...</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
          chatScrollArea.appendChild(aiBubble);
          chatScrollArea.scrollTop = chatScrollArea.scrollHeight;
        }, 600);

        chatScrollArea.scrollTop = chatScrollArea.scrollHeight;
      });
    }

    if (clearBtn && chatScrollArea) {
      clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        chatScrollArea.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-secondary text-on-secondary flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">psychology</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 max-w-[85%]">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-semibold">دستیار کارن AI</span>
                <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">اکنون</span>
              </div>
              <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md leading-relaxed">
                گفتگو بازنشانی شد. آماده‌ام تا برای سفر جدید، پروازها و بهترین هتل‌ها همراهیت کنم!
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });
    }

    function escapeHtml(string) {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.innerText = string;
      return div.innerHTML;
    }
  })();
</script></main><footer class="w-full bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface mt-space-xl pt-space-xl pb-space-lg rounded-t-[36px]"><div class="max-w-[1320px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop"><div <span>فاصله هتل تا فرودگاه:</span>
<span class="font-semibold text-on-surface">۱۸ دقیقه با خودرو (ترانسفر رایگان هتل)</span>

<script>
  (function() {
    const chatForm = document.getElementById('chatForm');
    const chatInput = document.getElementById('chatInput');
    const chatScrollArea = document.getElementById('chatScrollArea');
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearChatBtn');
    const chipPrompts = document.querySelectorAll('.chip-prompt');

    // Fill textarea with clicked prompt chip
    chipPrompts.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        chatInput.value = chip.textContent.trim();
        chatInput.focus();
      });
    });

    // Handle new message submit
    if (chatForm && chatInput && chatScrollArea) {
      chatForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const text = chatInput.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        // Append user bubble
        const now = new Date();
        const timeStr = now.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0') + ':' + now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0');
        
        const userBubble = document.createElement('div');
        userBubble.className = 'flex items-start justify-end gap-3';
        userBubble.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5 max-w-[85%]">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">${timeStr}</span>
              <span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-semibold">کیان احمدی</span>
            </div>
            <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none bg-primary text-on-primary font-body-md text-body-md leading-relaxed shadow-md">
              ${escapeHtml(text)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-label-lg font-bold">
            ک‌ا
          </div>
        `;
        chatScrollArea.appendChild(userBubble);
        chatInput.value = '';

        // Simulate AI thinking & reply
        setTimeout(() => {
          const aiBubble = document.createElement('div');
          aiBubble.className = 'flex items-start gap-3';
          aiBubble.innerHTML = `
            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-secondary text-on-secondary flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">psychology</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 max-w-[85%]">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-semibold">دستیار کارن AI</span>
                <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">${timeStr}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md leading-relaxed shadow-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-[18px] animate-spin">sync</span>
                <span>در حال تحلیل گزینه‌ها و استعلام ظرفیت پروازها بر اساس درخواست شما...</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
          chatScrollArea.appendChild(aiBubble);
          chatScrollArea.scrollTop = chatScrollArea.scrollHeight;
        }, 600);

        chatScrollArea.scrollTop = chatScrollArea.scrollHeight;
      });
    }

    if (clearBtn && chatScrollArea) {
      clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        chatScrollArea.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-secondary text-on-secondary flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">psychology</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 max-w-[85%]">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-semibold">دستیار کارن AI</span>
                <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">اکنون</span>
              </div>
              <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md leading-relaxed">
                گفتگو بازنشانی شد. آماده‌ام تا برای سفر جدید، پروازها و بهترین هتل‌ها همراهیت کنم!
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });
    }

    function escapeHtml(string) {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.innerText = string;
      return div.innerHTML;
    }
  })();
</script></main><footer class="w-full bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface mt-space-xl pt-space-xl pb-space-lg rounded-t-[36px]"><div class="max-w-[1320px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop"><div
